    public abstract class Token
    {
        public string Key { get; }
        public abstract object? ValueFor(Microsoft.Maui.ApplicationModel.AppTheme theme);
        public static void Apply(Microsoft.Maui.Controls.BindableObject target, Microsoft.Maui.Controls.BindableProperty property, PureMarkup.Maui.Token token) { }
    }
    public sealed class Token<TValue> : PureMarkup.Maui.Token
    {
        public Token(TValue value, string? name = null) { }
        public Token(TValue light, TValue dark, string? name = null) { }
        public TValue Dark { get; }
        public TValue Light { get; }
        public override object? ValueFor(Microsoft.Maui.ApplicationModel.AppTheme theme) { }
    }
